杩佺Щ楠屾敹 SQL锛?
```sql
select table_name
from information_schema.tables
where table_schema = 'public'
order by table_name;

select indexname, tablename
from pg_indexes
where schemaname = 'public'
  and (
    indexname like 'uk_%'
    or indexname like 'idx_%'
  )
order by tablename, indexname;
```

| `poi_id` | varchar(128) | 鑵捐 POI ID锛屽彲涓虹┖ |
| `poi_title` | varchar(128) | 鍦扮偣鍚嶇О |
| `map_raw_json` | jsonb | 鑵捐杩斿洖鍘熷鏁版嵁锛屼究浜庢帓鏌?|

寤鸿鏂板缂撳瓨琛紝鍑忓皯閲嶅璋冪敤鑵捐鎺ュ彛锛?
```sql
create table map_geocode_cache (
  id bigserial primary key,
  provider varchar(32) not null default 'tencent',
  cache_key varchar(128) not null,
  request_type varchar(32) not null,
  longitude decimal(10,6),
  latitude decimal(10,6),
  address varchar(255),
  response_json jsonb not null,
  expire_at timestamptz not null,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  constraint uk_map_geocode_cache unique(provider, cache_key, request_type)
);

create index idx_map_geocode_cache_expire on map_geocode_cache(expire_at);
```

```sql
create table wechat_subscribe_templates (
  id bigserial primary key,
  scene_code varchar(64) not null unique,
  template_id varchar(128) not null,
  title varchar(128) not null,
  status varchar(32) not null default 'active',
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

alter table notifications
  add column if not exists push_channel varchar(32) default 'in_app',
  add column if not exists wechat_template_id varchar(128),
  add column if not exists wechat_push_status varchar(32),
  add column if not exists wechat_push_result_json jsonb;
```

`users` 澧炲姞瀛楁锛?

| --- | --- | --- |
| `identity_bind_status` | varchar(32) | `pending/phone_bound/phone_verified/face_verified/verified/failed` |
| `phone_verified_status` | varchar(32) | `none/pending/pass/fail` |
| `face_verified_status` | varchar(32) | `none/pending/pass/fail` |
| `wechat_realname_consistency_status` | varchar(32) | `not_supported/pending/pass/fail` |

鏂板 `identity_verification_records`锛?
```sql
create table identity_verification_records (
  id bigserial primary key,
  user_id bigint not null,
  provider varchar(32) not null default 'tencent_faceid',
  verify_scene varchar(64) not null,
  real_name_encrypted text not null,
  id_card_no_encrypted text not null,
  id_card_no_masked varchar(32) not null,
  face_verify_status varchar(32) not null,
  face_score numeric(8,4),
  liveness_status varchar(32),
  biz_token varchar(128),
  rule_id varchar(128),
  provider_order_no varchar(128),
  provider_result_json jsonb,
  wechat_realname_consistency_status varchar(32) not null default 'not_supported',
  fail_reason text,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  verified_at timestamptz
);

create index idx_identity_verify_user_time on identity_verification_records(user_id, created_at);
```

鏂板 `phone_verification_records`锛?
```sql
create table phone_verification_records (
  id bigserial primary key,
  user_id bigint not null,
  provider varchar(32) not null default 'tencent_faceid',
  phone_encrypted text not null,
  phone_masked varchar(32) not null,
  verify_type varchar(32) not null,
  real_name_encrypted text,
  id_card_no_encrypted text,
  verify_status varchar(32) not null,
  provider_result_json jsonb,
  fail_reason text,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  verified_at timestamptz
);

create index idx_phone_verify_user_time on phone_verification_records(user_id, created_at);
```

#### C17.2.4 鏁版嵁搴?
```sql
create table sms_verification_codes (
  id bigserial primary key,
  scene varchar(64) not null,
  target_type varchar(32) not null,
  target_id bigint,
  phone_encrypted text not null,
  phone_masked varchar(32) not null,
  code_hash varchar(128) not null,
  provider varchar(32) not null default 'tencent_sms',
  template_id varchar(64) not null,
  send_status varchar(32) not null,
  verify_status varchar(32) not null default 'pending',
  request_id varchar(128),
  provider_serial_no varchar(128),
  provider_result_json jsonb,
  fail_reason text,
  expire_at timestamptz not null,
  verified_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

create index idx_sms_codes_target_scene on sms_verification_codes(target_type, target_id, scene, created_at);
create index idx_sms_codes_phone_time on sms_verification_codes(phone_masked, created_at);
create index idx_sms_codes_expire on sms_verification_codes(expire_at);
```

```sql
create table sms_send_rate_limits (
  id bigserial primary key,
  phone_hash varchar(128) not null,
  scene varchar(64) not null,
  send_date date not null,
  send_count int not null default 0,
  last_send_at timestamptz,
  constraint uk_sms_rate_phone_scene_date unique(phone_hash, scene, send_date)
);
```

```sql
  refund_notify_url varchar(255),
  status varchar(32) not null default 'inactive',
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
```

```sql
create table payment_callbacks (
  id bigserial primary key,
  provider varchar(32) not null default 'wechat_pay',
  callback_type varchar(64) not null,
  out_trade_no varchar(128),
  transaction_id varchar(128),
  raw_headers_json jsonb,
  raw_body text,
  verify_status varchar(32) not null,
  process_status varchar(32) not null,
  error_message text,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  processed_at timestamptz
);

create index idx_payment_callbacks_trade on payment_callbacks(out_trade_no, callback_type);
```

```sql
create table profitsharing_receivers (
  id bigserial primary key,
  user_id bigint,
  receiver_type varchar(32) not null,
  receiver_account varchar(128) not null,
  receiver_name_encrypted text,
  receiver_name_masked varchar(128),
  relation_type varchar(64),
  custom_relation varchar(64),
  provider varchar(32) not null default 'wechat_pay',
  provider_receiver_id varchar(128),
  status varchar(32) not null default 'pending',
  provider_result_json jsonb,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
```

```sql
create table profitsharing_orders (
  id bigserial primary key,
  revenue_record_id bigint not null,
  out_order_no varchar(128) not null unique,
  transaction_id varchar(128),
  provider_order_id varchar(128),
  status varchar(32) not null,
  amount_cent bigint not null default 0,
  provider_result_json jsonb,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  finished_at timestamptz
);
```

```sql
create table profitsharing_items (
  id bigserial primary key,
  profitsharing_order_id bigint not null,
  receiver_id bigint not null,
  user_id bigint,
  amount_cent bigint not null,
  description varchar(255),
  status varchar(32) not null,
  provider_detail_id varchar(128),
  provider_result_json jsonb,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
```

```sql
create table profitsharing_returns (
  id bigserial primary key,
  profitsharing_order_id bigint not null,
  out_return_no varchar(128) not null unique,
  return_mch_id varchar(64),
  amount_cent bigint not null,
  description varchar(255),
  status varchar(32) not null,
  provider_result_json jsonb,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  finished_at timestamptz
);
```

```sql
create table payment_reconciliation_tasks (
  id bigserial primary key,
  bill_date date not null,
  bill_type varchar(32) not null,
  provider varchar(32) not null default 'wechat_pay',
  status varchar(32) not null default 'pending',
  bill_file_id bigint,
  diff_count int not null default 0,
  diff_amount_cent bigint not null default 0,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  finished_at timestamptz
);
```

```sql
create table payment_reconciliation_diffs (
  id bigserial primary key,
  task_id bigint not null,
  out_trade_no varchar(128),
  transaction_id varchar(128),
  diff_type varchar(64) not null,
  local_amount_cent bigint,
  provider_amount_cent bigint,
  local_status varchar(32),
  provider_status varchar(32),
  handle_status varchar(32) not null default 'pending',
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  handled_at timestamptz
);
```

```sql
create table game_milestones (
  id bigserial primary key,
  game_id bigint not null,
  title varchar(100) not null,
  description text,
  status varchar(32) not null default 'pending',
  due_time timestamptz,
  completed_at timestamptz,
  created_by bigint not null,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

create index idx_game_milestones_game_status on game_milestones(game_id, status);
```

```sql
create table game_checkins (
  id bigserial primary key,
  game_id bigint not null,
  milestone_id bigint,
  user_id bigint not null,
  checkin_type varchar(32) not null,
  content text,
  file_ids jsonb not null default '[]'::jsonb,
  latitude decimal(10,7),
  longitude decimal(10,7),
  status varchar(32) not null default 'valid',
  invalid_reason text,
  invalid_admin_id bigint,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

create index idx_game_checkins_game_created on game_checkins(game_id, created_at desc);
create index idx_game_checkins_user_created on game_checkins(user_id, created_at desc);
```

```sql
create table game_retrospectives (
  id bigserial primary key,
  game_id bigint not null,
  user_id bigint not null,
  summary text not null,
  highlights jsonb not null default '[]'::jsonb,
  problems jsonb not null default '[]'::jsonb,
  next_intent varchar(32),
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  unique (game_id, user_id)
);

create index idx_game_retrospectives_game on game_retrospectives(game_id);
```

```sql
);
```

```sql
create table points_accounts (
  id bigserial primary key,
  user_id bigint not null unique,
  available_points int not null default 0,
  frozen_points int not null default 0,
  total_earned_points int not null default 0,
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

create table points_logs (
  id bigserial primary key,
  user_id bigint not null,
  change_value int not null,
  before_points int not null,
  after_points int not null,
  biz_type varchar(32) not null,
  biz_id bigint,
  reason text,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

create index idx_points_logs_user_created on points_logs(user_id, created_at desc);
```

```sql
create table redemption_items (
  id bigserial primary key,
  name varchar(100) not null,
  description text,
  points_cost int not null,
  stock int not null default 0,
  status varchar(32) not null default 'active',
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

create table redemption_orders (
  id bigserial primary key,
  order_no varchar(64) not null unique,
  user_id bigint not null,
  item_id bigint not null,
  points_cost int not null,
  status varchar(32) not null default 'pending',
  review_admin_id bigint,
  review_reason text,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

create index idx_redemption_orders_user_created on redemption_orders(user_id, created_at desc);
```

```sql
create table team_relations (
  id bigserial primary key,
  leader_user_id bigint not null,
  member_user_id bigint not null,
  relation_level int not null default 1,
  source varchar(32) not null,
  status varchar(32) not null default 'active',
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  unique (leader_user_id, member_user_id, relation_level)
);

create table member_report_snapshots (
  id bigserial primary key,
  user_id bigint not null,
  report_type varchar(32) not null,
  period varchar(32) not null,
  metrics jsonb not null default '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

create index idx_member_report_snapshots_user_period on member_report_snapshots(user_id, report_type, period);
```

```sql
create table delivery_documents (
  id bigserial primary key,
  doc_type varchar(64) not null,
  title varchar(200) not null,
  file_id bigint,
  version varchar(32) not null default 'v1',
  status varchar(32) not null default 'draft',
  remark text,
  created_by_admin_id bigint not null,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

create table test_cases (
  id bigserial primary key,
  module varchar(64) not null,
  case_name varchar(200) not null,
  priority varchar(16) not null,
  steps jsonb not null default '[]'::jsonb,
  expected_result text not null,
  status varchar(32) not null default 'active',
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

create table test_runs (
  id bigserial primary key,
  case_id bigint not null,
  environment varchar(32) not null,
  result varchar(32) not null,
  actual_result text,
  request_id varchar(128),
  evidence_file_ids jsonb not null default '[]'::jsonb,
  runner_admin_id bigint,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

create index idx_test_runs_case_created on test_runs(case_id, created_at desc);
```
